docs/findings/OPEN.md:85:| OOH2-RT-H1 | High (AEGIS runtime omega7 H-1; Ω RED at 4a0a4b4 kept it High; DATA graded the same behaviour M-1 Medium) | next OOH wave; blocks merge and production | 2026-10-18 | ooh-py store.py:180-184 `append_prepared` compares only the log file's size with memory before a write, and the running service never re-reads the file (`RecordLog.verify()` has no caller; `verify_integrity` checks the ledger against the in-memory line hashes). A same-size in-place edit of an existing log line while the service runs is written over: writes answer 201 and integrity reports ok; only a restart refuses (StoreCorrupt or "no matching anchor"), and acknowledged lines can end up behind a broken chain. Contradicts store.py:159-160 ("the file must be exactly the in-memory lines before the write"), ADR 0022 (wave-2 section, "before a write the file must be exactly memory") and the docstring of tests/test_aegis_store_write_path.py (DATA INFO-7).
